## Commit 3 — `147b361f` — "Sync About story across locales and align Spanish rental copy"
**Files:** All 4 locales. 188 insertions / 242 deletions.

### What it does

This commit **propagates the new about-page narrative from commit 2 into en.json, nl.json, and de.json**, plus a few small copy-alignment tweaks.

The English about-page, which previously said:
> "We are Sonia and Luana Weiss van der Pol. Dutch women living in Paraguay for 7+ years. We founded Nexa after processing each step of the migration process ourselves."

now reads:
> "Nexa Paraguay was founded with a single mission: to transform the relocation process from a bureaucratic hurdle into a welcoming experience."

Same for NL and DE — the new "experience turned into expertise" / "Ervaring omgezet in kennis" / "Erfahrung wird zu Wissen" framing replaces the old "Sonia and Luana, Dutch women for 7+ years" story.

### Sonia's bio across all locales

The stale bio ("Paraguayan, lived 7 years in the Netherlands. Founder of Nexa Paraguay and creator of the Dutch community 'Proyecto Paraguay'.") got replaced with the new factual one ("Sonia is Paraguayan and married to a Dutch national...") — including in EN/NL/DE.

### Luana's bio across all locales

The stale bio ("Daughter of Sonia, born in the Netherlands. Speaks Dutch, English, and Spanish.") was replaced with the new one ("Luana coordinates each case end to end...") — same change in EN/NL/DE.

### es.json small copy alignments (the only es.json edits)

Two minor tweaks to keep the rental copy consistent:

- `/servicios` (homepage rentals): "Búsqueda y negociación de propiedades en Asunción y área metropolitana." → "Buscamos propiedades en el territorio paraguayo según tu presupuesto y preferencias." (matches commit 1's homepage rental edit)

- `/para-holandeses` page hero subheadline: "Sonia y Luana son holandesas. 7 años viviendo el proceso. Acompañamos holandeses..." → "Sonia es paraguaya y está casada con un ciudadano neerlandés; Luana coordina cada caso. Acompañamos a holandeses..."

Plus a stray comma fix in the FAQ: `}, ` (extra trailing space) → `},`.

## Summary table

| Theme | What Kiki changed |
|---|---|
| **Honesty in copy** | Stripped all four hero "stats" (80+, 4.8/5, 8-12 sem., USD fixed price) — they were aspirational, not factual. Same for the about-page "Trust in numbers" block. Removed "6x más rápido" / "14 meses" / "USD 11.000" highlights that came from one founder's anecdotal experience. |
| **Founder facts corrected** | Sonia is **Paraguayan married to a Dutchman** — not a Dutchwoman. Luana is Sonia's **daughter** — not her sister. The page had a stale surname "Wehrli" that should be "Weiss van der Pol". The "Dutch women 7+ years in Paraguay" framing is gone. |
| **Geography expanded** | Rental copy now says "territorio paraguayo" instead of "Asunción y área metropolitana" — they help with properties beyond the capital. |
| **Tone tightened** | Friendly "vos" register replaced with neutral professional "usted/te". "Te acompañamos a sacar tu cédula. Salís con el documento, no con promesas." → "Te ayudamos a sacar tu cédula." Pattern repeats in banking, RUC, and rental descriptions. |
| **FAQ pruned** | Removed "¿Qué incluye el precio?" and "¿Puedo pagar en cuotas?" — these leaked too much info before a consultation. Reworked "¿Cuánto cuesta?" to push to free consultation. |
| **About-page narrative** | Replaced "Our story is we did it ourselves" with "Our mission is to make relocation a welcoming experience, not a bureaucratic hurdle" — softer, more service-oriented. |
| **Credentials removed** | Sonia's 3-line credentials (Dutch law degree, 7 years in PY, SEPRELAD inscription) deleted. Just "Fundadora" + bio. |
| **Locale parity** | The new about-page narrative + corrected bios synced into EN/NL/DE — they were still on the old "Dutch women 7+ years" version. |

## What it adds up to

Kiki's commits take the site from a **marketed-with-claims** version to a **softer, honest, factual** version. The main thing you'd notice as a visitor:
- **No big "80+ clientes / 4.8/5" stats** in the hero anymore
- **No "6x más rápido" highlight card** on the about page
- The about story reads as a Paraguayan founder who learned the system through her own family, not as Dutch expats who moved there
- Spanish service copy is cleaner and more formal (no more "no con promesas" colloquialisms)

Everything else (SEO, structure, sections, PWA, Traefik routing) is untouched. The change is purely the words.
